             SUM(CASE WHEN date >= '2025-01-01' AND date < '2026-01-01' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS net_cash_flow_2025

SQL: WITH

             AVG(target_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS avg_rebalance_gap

    )
    , 
    scen AS (

             AVG(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS avg_scenario_change,

      FROM ATOM_EVENT_SCENARIO_REBALANCING_001
      GROUP BY investor_id

wealth_management_multi_step_comparative_105_questions:MC-026 MISMATCH
Across risk_tolerance, how do cash-flow net amount, scenario change, and scenario count compare using three related tables?
The pipeline returns the risk-tolerance categories but uses cash-flow totals and raw scenario counts instead of the required average net cash flow and average scenario count. Its scenario-change averages also do not match the reference, and the null group is incorrectly split into separate rows.
SQL: WITH 
    cash AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS transaction_count,
             SUM(amount) AS net_cash_flow,
             SUM(CASE WHEN amount > 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS inflow_amount,
             -SUM(CASE WHEN amount < 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS outflow_amount,
             SUM(CASE WHEN date >= '2025-01-01' AND date < '2026-01-01' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS net_cash_flow_2025
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_CASH_FLOW_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , 
    scen AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS scenario_count,
             AVG(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS avg_scenario_change,
             MAX(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS max_scenario_change
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_SCENARIO_REBALANCING_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
     SELECT p.risk_tolerance AS group_value, ROUND(AVG(cash.net_cash_flow),2) AS avg_net_cash_flow, ROUND(AVG(scen.avg_scenario_change),2) AS avg_scenario_change, ROUND(AVG(scen.scenario_count),2) AS avg_scenario_count FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTOR_PROFILE_001 p JOIN cash ON cash.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN scen ON scen.investor_id=p.investor_id GROUP BY p.risk_tolerance;

Across time_horizon, how do cash-flow net amount, scenario change, and scenario count compare using three related tables?
The pipeline returns totals and raw counts for only three non-null time horizons, while the ground truth requires average net cash flow, average scenario change, and average scenario count across four groups including the null time_horizon group.
SQL: WITH 
    cash AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS transaction_count,
             SUM(amount) AS net_cash_flow,
             SUM(CASE WHEN amount > 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS inflow_amount,
             -SUM(CASE WHEN amount < 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS outflow_amount,
             SUM(CASE WHEN date >= '2025-01-01' AND date < '2026-01-01' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS net_cash_flow_2025
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_CASH_FLOW_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , 
    scen AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS scenario_count,
             AVG(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS avg_scenario_change,
             MAX(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS max_scenario_change
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_SCENARIO_REBALANCING_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
     SELECT p.time_horizon AS group_value, ROUND(AVG(cash.net_cash_flow),2) AS avg_net_cash_flow, ROUND(AVG(scen.avg_scenario_change),2) AS avg_scenario_change, ROUND(AVG(scen.scenario_count),2) AS avg_scenario_count FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTOR_PROFILE_001 p JOIN cash ON cash.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN scen ON scen.investor_id=p.investor_id GROUP BY p.time_horizon;

SQL: WITH 
    alloc AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS allocation_count,
             MAX(allocation_pct) AS max_allocation_pct,
             AVG(allocation_pct) AS avg_allocation_pct,
             SUM(total_investment) AS total_sector_investment
      FROM ATOM_ENTITY_SECTOR_ALLOCATION_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , 
    holding AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS holding_count,
             SUM(current_value) AS total_holding_value,
             SUM(current_value-cost) AS total_holding_gain,
             AVG(returns_pct) AS avg_returns_pct,
             SUM(dividends) AS total_dividends,
             SUM(taxes_paid) AS total_taxes_paid
      FROM ATOM_ENTITY_PORTFOLIO_HOLDING_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
     SELECT p.category AS group_value, ROUND(AVG(alloc.max_allocation_pct),2) AS avg_max_allocation_pct, ROUND(AVG(holding.avg_returns_pct),2) AS avg_returns_pct, ROUND(AVG(holding.total_holding_value),2) AS avg_holding_value FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTOR_PROFILE_001 p JOIN alloc ON alloc.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN holding ON holding.investor_id=p.investor_id GROUP BY p.category;

wealth_management_multi_step_comparative_105_questions:MC-039 PARTIAL
Across profile category, how do rebalancing amount, cash flow, and transaction count compare using three related tables?
The pipeline returns the correct profile-category groups and related measures, but it reports totals rather than the required average net cash flow, average transaction count, and average rebalance amount shown in the ground truth.
SQL: WITH 
    cash AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS transaction_count,
             SUM(amount) AS net_cash_flow,
             SUM(CASE WHEN amount > 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS inflow_amount,
             -SUM(CASE WHEN amount < 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS outflow_amount,
             SUM(CASE WHEN date >= '2025-01-01' AND date < '2026-01-01' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS net_cash_flow_2025
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_CASH_FLOW_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , 
    reb AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS rebalance_action_count,
             SUM(amount) AS total_rebalance_amount,
             AVG(target_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS avg_rebalance_gap
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_REBALANCING_ACTION_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
     SELECT p.category AS group_value, ROUND(AVG(cash.net_cash_flow),2) AS avg_net_cash_flow, ROUND(AVG(cash.transaction_count),2) AS avg_transaction_count, ROUND(AVG(reb.total_rebalance_amount),2) AS avg_rebalance_amount FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTOR_PROFILE_001 p JOIN cash ON cash.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN reb ON reb.investor_id=p.investor_id GROUP BY p.category;

             SUM(CASE WHEN date >= '2025-01-01' AND date < '2026-01-01' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS net_cash_flow_2025

             COUNT(*) AS scenario_count,
             AVG(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS avg_scenario_change,

SQL: WITH 
    cash AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS transaction_count,
             SUM(amount) AS net_cash_flow,
             SUM(CASE WHEN amount > 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS inflow_amount,
             -SUM(CASE WHEN amount < 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS outflow_amount,
             SUM(CASE WHEN date >= '2025-01-01' AND date < '2026-01-01' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS net_cash_flow_2025
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_CASH_FLOW_001

    )

wealth_management_multi_step_comparative_105_questions:MC-056 PARTIAL
Across risk_tolerance, how do cash flow, scenario change, allocation concentration, and sector investment compare using four related tables?
Cash flow and scenario change averages match for all risk-tolerance groups, and allocation concentration matches for Aggressive, Conservative, and Moderate. However, sector investment is reported as totals rather than the required averages for every group, and the null-group allocation concentration is incorrect.
SQL: WITH 
    cash AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS transaction_count,
             SUM(amount) AS net_cash_flow,
             SUM(CASE WHEN amount > 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS inflow_amount,
             -SUM(CASE WHEN amount < 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS outflow_amount,
             SUM(CASE WHEN date >= '2025-01-01' AND date < '2026-01-01' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS net_cash_flow_2025
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_CASH_FLOW_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , 
    scen AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS scenario_count,
             AVG(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS avg_scenario_change,
             MAX(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS max_scenario_change
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_SCENARIO_REBALANCING_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , 
    alloc AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS allocation_count,
             MAX(allocation_pct) AS max_allocation_pct,
             AVG(allocation_pct) AS avg_allocation_pct,
             SUM(total_investment) AS total_sector_investment
      FROM ATOM_ENTITY_SECTOR_ALLOCATION_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
     SELECT p.risk_tolerance AS group_value, ROUND(AVG(cash.net_cash_flow),2) AS avg_net_cash_flow, ROUND(AVG(scen.avg_scenario_change),2) AS avg_scenario_change, ROUND(AVG(alloc.max_allocation_pct),2) AS avg_max_allocation_pct, ROUND(AVG(alloc.total_sector_investment),2) AS avg_sector_investment FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTOR_PROFILE_001 p JOIN cash ON cash.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN scen ON scen.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN alloc ON alloc.investor_id=p.investor_id GROUP BY p.risk_tolerance;

wealth_management_multi_step_comparative_105_questions:MC-061 MATCH
Across time_horizon, how do goal progress, holding gain, risk score, and liquidity compare using four related tables?
All four time_horizon groups, including the null group, are present with semantically corresponding average goal progress, holding gain, risk score, and liquidity values matching the ground truth within tolerance.
SQL: WITH goal AS (SELECT investor_id, AVG(progress_pct) AS avg_progress_pct FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTMENT_GOAL_001 GROUP BY investor_id), 
    holding AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS holding_count,
             SUM(current_value) AS total_holding_value,
             SUM(current_value-cost) AS total_holding_gain,
             AVG(returns_pct) AS avg_returns_pct,
             SUM(dividends) AS total_dividends,
             SUM(taxes_paid) AS total_taxes_paid
      FROM ATOM_ENTITY_PORTFOLIO_HOLDING_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
     SELECT p.time_horizon AS group_value, ROUND(AVG(goal.avg_progress_pct),2) AS avg_progress_pct, ROUND(AVG(holding.total_holding_gain),2) AS avg_holding_gain, ROUND(AVG(h.risk_score),2) AS avg_risk_score, ROUND(AVG(h.liquidity_score),2) AS avg_liquidity_score FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTOR_PROFILE_001 p JOIN goal ON goal.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN holding ON holding.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN ATOM_ENTITY_PORTFOLIO_HEALTH_001 h ON h.investor_id=p.investor_id GROUP BY p.time_horizon;

wealth_management_multi_step_comparative_105_questions:MC-065 PARTIAL
Across time_horizon, how do scenario change, rebalancing gap, health risk, and goal match compare using four related tables?
All required time_horizon groups are present, and health risk and goal match averages are within tolerance. However, scenario change is incorrect for every group, and rebalancing gap is also materially incorrect for every group.
SQL: WITH 
    scen AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS scenario_count,
             AVG(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS avg_scenario_change,
             MAX(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS max_scenario_change
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_SCENARIO_REBALANCING_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , 
    reb AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS rebalance_action_count,
             SUM(amount) AS total_rebalance_amount,
             AVG(target_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS avg_rebalance_gap
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_REBALANCING_ACTION_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )

wealth_management_multi_step_comparative_105_questions:MC-066 MISMATCH
Across time_horizon, how do cash flow, scenario change, allocation concentration, and sector investment compare using four related tables?
The pipeline omits the null time_horizon group and the reported averages for all included horizons materially conflict with the ground truth, especially cash flow and sector investment.
SQL: WITH 
    cash AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS transaction_count,
             SUM(amount) AS net_cash_flow,
             SUM(CASE WHEN amount > 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS inflow_amount,
             -SUM(CASE WHEN amount < 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS outflow_amount,
             SUM(CASE WHEN date >= '2025-01-01' AND date < '2026-01-01' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS net_cash_flow_2025
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_CASH_FLOW_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , 
    scen AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS scenario_count,
             AVG(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS avg_scenario_change,
             MAX(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS max_scenario_change
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_SCENARIO_REBALANCING_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , 
    alloc AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS allocation_count,
             MAX(allocation_pct) AS max_allocation_pct,
             AVG(allocation_pct) AS avg_allocation_pct,
             SUM(total_investment) AS total_sector_investment
      FROM ATOM_ENTITY_SECTOR_ALLOCATION_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
     SELECT p.time_horizon AS group_value, ROUND(AVG(cash.net_cash_flow),2) AS avg_net_cash_flow, ROUND(AVG(scen.avg_scenario_change),2) AS avg_scenario_change, ROUND(AVG(alloc.max_allocation_pct),2) AS avg_max_allocation_pct, ROUND(AVG(alloc.total_sector_investment),2) AS avg_sector_investment FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTOR_PROFILE_001 p JOIN cash ON cash.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN scen ON scen.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN alloc ON alloc.investor_id=p.investor_id GROUP BY p.time_horizon;

SQL: WITH goal AS (SELECT investor_id, AVG(progress_pct) AS avg_progress_pct FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTMENT_GOAL_001 GROUP BY investor_id), 
    holding AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS holding_count,
             SUM(current_value) AS total_holding_value,
             SUM(current_value-cost) AS total_holding_gain,
             AVG(returns_pct) AS avg_returns_pct,
             SUM(dividends) AS total_dividends,
             SUM(taxes_paid) AS total_taxes_paid
      FROM ATOM_ENTITY_PORTFOLIO_HOLDING_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
     SELECT p.category AS group_value, ROUND(AVG(goal.avg_progress_pct),2) AS avg_progress_pct, ROUND(AVG(holding.total_holding_gain),2) AS avg_holding_gain, ROUND(AVG(h.risk_score),2) AS avg_risk_score, ROUND(AVG(h.liquidity_score),2) AS avg_liquidity_score FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTOR_PROFILE_001 p JOIN goal ON goal.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN holding ON holding.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN ATOM_ENTITY_PORTFOLIO_HEALTH_001 h ON h.investor_id=p.investor_id GROUP BY p.category;

wealth_management_multi_step_comparative_105_questions:MC-076 MISMATCH

SQL: WITH 
    cash AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS transaction_count,
             SUM(amount) AS net_cash_flow,
             SUM(CASE WHEN amount > 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS inflow_amount,
             -SUM(CASE WHEN amount < 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS outflow_amount,
             SUM(CASE WHEN date >= '2025-01-01' AND date < '2026-01-01' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS net_cash_flow_2025
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_CASH_FLOW_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , 
    scen AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS scenario_count,
             AVG(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS avg_scenario_change,
             MAX(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS max_scenario_change
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_SCENARIO_REBALANCING_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , 
    alloc AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS allocation_count,
             MAX(allocation_pct) AS max_allocation_pct,
             AVG(allocation_pct) AS avg_allocation_pct,
             SUM(total_investment) AS total_sector_investment
      FROM ATOM_ENTITY_SECTOR_ALLOCATION_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
     SELECT p.category AS group_value, ROUND(AVG(cash.net_cash_flow),2) AS avg_net_cash_flow, ROUND(AVG(scen.avg_scenario_change),2) AS avg_scenario_change, ROUND(AVG(alloc.max_allocation_pct),2) AS avg_max_allocation_pct, ROUND(AVG(alloc.total_sector_investment),2) AS avg_sector_investment FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTOR_PROFILE_001 p JOIN cash ON cash.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN scen ON scen.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN alloc ON alloc.investor_id=p.investor_id GROUP BY p.category;

SQL: WITH goal AS (SELECT investor_id, AVG(shortfall) AS avg_shortfall FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTMENT_GOAL_001 GROUP BY investor_id), 
    alloc AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS allocation_count,
             MAX(allocation_pct) AS max_allocation_pct,
             AVG(allocation_pct) AS avg_allocation_pct,
             SUM(total_investment) AS total_sector_investment
      FROM ATOM_ENTITY_SECTOR_ALLOCATION_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
     SELECT p.category AS group_value, ROUND(AVG(goal.avg_shortfall),2) AS avg_shortfall, ROUND(AVG(alloc.max_allocation_pct),2) AS avg_max_allocation_pct, ROUND(AVG(h.liquidity_score),2) AS avg_liquidity_score, ROUND(AVG(h.diversification_score),2) AS avg_diversification_score FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTOR_PROFILE_001 p JOIN goal ON goal.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN alloc ON alloc.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN ATOM_ENTITY_PORTFOLIO_HEALTH_001 h ON h.investor_id=p.investor_id GROUP BY p.category;

wealth_management_multi_step_comparative_105_questions:MC-083 PARTIAL
Across risk_tolerance, how do goal shortfall, holding value, allocation concentration, liquidity, and diversification compare using five related tables?
All risk_tolerance groups are present and the holding value, allocation concentration, liquidity, and diversification averages match the ground truth. However, avg_shortfall is materially incorrect for every group.
SQL: WITH goal AS (SELECT investor_id, AVG(shortfall) AS avg_shortfall FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTMENT_GOAL_001 GROUP BY investor_id), 
    holding AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS holding_count,
             SUM(current_value) AS total_holding_value,
             SUM(current_value-cost) AS total_holding_gain,
             AVG(returns_pct) AS avg_returns_pct,
             SUM(dividends) AS total_dividends,
             SUM(taxes_paid) AS total_taxes_paid
      FROM ATOM_ENTITY_PORTFOLIO_HOLDING_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , 
    alloc AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS allocation_count,
             MAX(allocation_pct) AS max_allocation_pct,
             AVG(allocation_pct) AS avg_allocation_pct,
             SUM(total_investment) AS total_sector_investment
      FROM ATOM_ENTITY_SECTOR_ALLOCATION_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )

wealth_management_multi_step_comparative_105_questions:MC-084 MISMATCH
Across risk_tolerance, how do cash flow, rebalancing amount, scenario change, risk score, and goal match compare using five related tables?
The pipeline omits the required null risk_tolerance group and its averages materially differ from the ground truth for all reported risk_tolerance groups across key requested metrics.
SQL: WITH 
    cash AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS transaction_count,
             SUM(amount) AS net_cash_flow,
             SUM(CASE WHEN amount > 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS inflow_amount,
             -SUM(CASE WHEN amount < 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS outflow_amount,
             SUM(CASE WHEN date >= '2025-01-01' AND date < '2026-01-01' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS net_cash_flow_2025
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_CASH_FLOW_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , 
    reb AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS rebalance_action_count,
             SUM(amount) AS total_rebalance_amount,
             AVG(target_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS avg_rebalance_gap
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_REBALANCING_ACTION_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , 
    scen AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS scenario_count,
             AVG(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS avg_scenario_change,
             MAX(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS max_scenario_change
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_SCENARIO_REBALANCING_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
     SELECT p.risk_tolerance AS group_value, ROUND(AVG(cash.net_cash_flow),2) AS avg_net_cash_flow, ROUND(AVG(reb.total_rebalance_amount),2) AS avg_rebalance_amount, ROUND(AVG(scen.avg_scenario_change),2) AS avg_scenario_change, ROUND(AVG(h.risk_score),2) AS avg_risk_score, ROUND(AVG(h.goal_match_pct),2) AS avg_goal_match_pct FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTOR_PROFILE_001 p JOIN cash ON cash.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN reb ON reb.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN scen ON scen.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN ATOM_ENTITY_PORTFOLIO_HEALTH_001 h ON h.investor_id=p.investor_id GROUP BY p.risk_tolerance;

wealth_management_multi_step_comparative_105_questions:MC-086 MISMATCH
Across risk_tolerance, how do cash-flow inflow, outflow, goal shortfall, holding dividends, and taxes paid compare using five related tables?
The pipeline groups inflows and outflows by transaction type rather than risk_tolerance, and its risk-tolerance rows omit inflow/outflow and contain incorrect shortfall, dividend, and tax averages.
SQL: WITH 
    cash AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS transaction_count,
             SUM(amount) AS net_cash_flow,
             SUM(CASE WHEN amount > 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS inflow_amount,
             -SUM(CASE WHEN amount < 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS outflow_amount,
             SUM(CASE WHEN date >= '2025-01-01' AND date < '2026-01-01' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS net_cash_flow_2025
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_CASH_FLOW_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , goal AS (SELECT investor_id, AVG(shortfall) AS avg_shortfall FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTMENT_GOAL_001 GROUP BY investor_id), 
    holding AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS holding_count,
             SUM(current_value) AS total_holding_value,
             SUM(current_value-cost) AS total_holding_gain,
             AVG(returns_pct) AS avg_returns_pct,
             SUM(dividends) AS total_dividends,
             SUM(taxes_paid) AS total_taxes_paid
      FROM ATOM_ENTITY_PORTFOLIO_HOLDING_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
     SELECT p.risk_tolerance AS group_value, ROUND(AVG(cash.inflow_amount),2) AS avg_inflow, ROUND(AVG(cash.outflow_amount),2) AS avg_outflow, ROUND(AVG(goal.avg_shortfall),2) AS avg_shortfall, ROUND(AVG(holding.total_dividends),2) AS avg_dividends, ROUND(AVG(holding.total_taxes_paid),2) AS avg_taxes_paid FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTOR_PROFILE_001 p JOIN cash ON cash.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN goal ON goal.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN holding ON holding.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN ATOM_ENTITY_PORTFOLIO_HEALTH_001 h ON h.investor_id=p.investor_id GROUP BY p.risk_tolerance;

wealth_management_multi_step_comparative_105_questions:MC-090 PARTIAL
Across time_horizon, how do goal shortfall, holding value, allocation concentration, liquidity, and diversification compare using five related tables?
The pipeline correctly reports all five requested average metrics for Long-term, Medium-term, and Short-term horizons, but omits the required null/unspecified time_horizon group present in the ground truth.
SQL: WITH goal AS (SELECT investor_id, AVG(shortfall) AS avg_shortfall FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTMENT_GOAL_001 GROUP BY investor_id), 
    holding AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS holding_count,
             SUM(current_value) AS total_holding_value,
             SUM(current_value-cost) AS total_holding_gain,
             AVG(returns_pct) AS avg_returns_pct,
             SUM(dividends) AS total_dividends,
             SUM(taxes_paid) AS total_taxes_paid
      FROM ATOM_ENTITY_PORTFOLIO_HOLDING_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , 
    alloc AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS allocation_count,
             MAX(allocation_pct) AS max_allocation_pct,
             AVG(allocation_pct) AS avg_allocation_pct,
             SUM(total_investment) AS total_sector_investment
      FROM ATOM_ENTITY_SECTOR_ALLOCATION_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
     SELECT p.time_horizon AS group_value, ROUND(AVG(goal.avg_shortfall),2) AS avg_shortfall, ROUND(AVG(holding.total_holding_value),2) AS avg_holding_value, ROUND(AVG(alloc.max_allocation_pct),2) AS avg_max_allocation_pct, ROUND(AVG(h.liquidity_score),2) AS avg_liquidity_score, ROUND(AVG(h.diversification_score),2) AS avg_diversification_score FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTOR_PROFILE_001 p JOIN goal ON goal.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN holding ON holding.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN alloc ON alloc.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN ATOM_ENTITY_PORTFOLIO_HEALTH_001 h ON h.investor_id=p.investor_id GROUP BY p.time_horizon;

wealth_management_multi_step_comparative_105_questions:MC-091 MISMATCH
Across time_horizon, how do cash flow, rebalancing amount, scenario change, risk score, and goal match compare using five related tables?
The pipeline includes the expected time_horizon groups but its cash-flow, rebalancing, scenario-change, risk-score, and goal-match averages materially conflict with the ground truth for every group.
SQL: WITH 
    cash AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS transaction_count,
             SUM(amount) AS net_cash_flow,
             SUM(CASE WHEN amount > 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS inflow_amount,
             -SUM(CASE WHEN amount < 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS outflow_amount,
             SUM(CASE WHEN date >= '2025-01-01' AND date < '2026-01-01' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS net_cash_flow_2025
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_CASH_FLOW_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , 
    reb AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS rebalance_action_count,
             SUM(amount) AS total_rebalance_amount,
             AVG(target_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS avg_rebalance_gap
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_REBALANCING_ACTION_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , 
    scen AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS scenario_count,
             AVG(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS avg_scenario_change,
             MAX(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS max_scenario_change
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_SCENARIO_REBALANCING_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
     SELECT p.time_horizon AS group_value, ROUND(AVG(cash.net_cash_flow),2) AS avg_net_cash_flow, ROUND(AVG(reb.total_rebalance_amount),2) AS avg_rebalance_amount, ROUND(AVG(scen.avg_scenario_change),2) AS avg_scenario_change, ROUND(AVG(h.risk_score),2) AS avg_risk_score, ROUND(AVG(h.goal_match_pct),2) AS avg_goal_match_pct FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTOR_PROFILE_001 p JOIN cash ON cash.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN reb ON reb.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN scen ON scen.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN ATOM_ENTITY_PORTFOLIO_HEALTH_001 h ON h.investor_id=p.investor_id GROUP BY p.time_horizon;

SQL: WITH 
    cash AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS transaction_count,
             SUM(amount) AS net_cash_flow,
             SUM(CASE WHEN amount > 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS inflow_amount,
             -SUM(CASE WHEN amount < 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS outflow_amount,
             SUM(CASE WHEN date >= '2025-01-01' AND date < '2026-01-01' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS net_cash_flow_2025

    , goal AS (SELECT investor_id, AVG(shortfall) AS avg_shortfall FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTMENT_GOAL_001 GROUP BY investor_id),

wealth_management_multi_step_comparative_105_questions:MC-098 MISMATCH
Across profile category, how do cash flow, rebalancing amount, scenario change, risk score, and goal match compare using five related tables?
The pipeline includes the same six profile-category groups, but its averages materially disagree with the ground truth for nearly every requested metric and category.
SQL: WITH 
    cash AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS transaction_count,
             SUM(amount) AS net_cash_flow,
             SUM(CASE WHEN amount > 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS inflow_amount,
             -SUM(CASE WHEN amount < 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS outflow_amount,
             SUM(CASE WHEN date >= '2025-01-01' AND date < '2026-01-01' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS net_cash_flow_2025
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_CASH_FLOW_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , 
    reb AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS rebalance_action_count,
             SUM(amount) AS total_rebalance_amount,
             AVG(target_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS avg_rebalance_gap
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_REBALANCING_ACTION_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , 
    scen AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS scenario_count,
             AVG(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS avg_scenario_change,
             MAX(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS max_scenario_change
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_SCENARIO_REBALANCING_001
      GROUP BY investor_id

wealth_management_multi_step_comparative_105_questions:MC-100 MISMATCH
Across profile category, how do cash-flow inflow, outflow, goal shortfall, holding dividends, and taxes paid compare using five related tables?
The pipeline reports totals rather than the required average metrics, omits the null/unclassified profile category, and presents outflows as negative totals rather than the ground-truth average outflows.
SQL: WITH 
    cash AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS transaction_count,
             SUM(amount) AS net_cash_flow,
             SUM(CASE WHEN amount > 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS inflow_amount,
             -SUM(CASE WHEN amount < 0 THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS outflow_amount,
             SUM(CASE WHEN date >= '2025-01-01' AND date < '2026-01-01' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS net_cash_flow_2025
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_CASH_FLOW_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , goal AS (SELECT investor_id, AVG(shortfall) AS avg_shortfall FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTMENT_GOAL_001 GROUP BY investor_id), 
    holding AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS holding_count,
             SUM(current_value) AS total_holding_value,
             SUM(current_value-cost) AS total_holding_gain,
             AVG(returns_pct) AS avg_returns_pct,
             SUM(dividends) AS total_dividends,
             SUM(taxes_paid) AS total_taxes_paid
      FROM ATOM_ENTITY_PORTFOLIO_HOLDING_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
     SELECT p.category AS group_value, ROUND(AVG(cash.inflow_amount),2) AS avg_inflow, ROUND(AVG(cash.outflow_amount),2) AS avg_outflow, ROUND(AVG(goal.avg_shortfall),2) AS avg_shortfall, ROUND(AVG(holding.total_dividends),2) AS avg_dividends, ROUND(AVG(holding.total_taxes_paid),2) AS avg_taxes_paid FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTOR_PROFILE_001 p JOIN cash ON cash.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN goal ON goal.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN holding ON holding.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN ATOM_ENTITY_PORTFOLIO_HEALTH_001 h ON h.investor_id=p.investor_id GROUP BY p.category;

wealth_management_multi_step_comparative_105_questions:MC-104 MISMATCH

The pipeline returns a substantially different top-ten investor set and incorrect risk-pressure scores. Only two ground-truth investors appear, and neither has the correct score or ranking.
SQL: WITH 
    alloc AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS allocation_count,
             MAX(allocation_pct) AS max_allocation_pct,
             AVG(allocation_pct) AS avg_allocation_pct,
             SUM(total_investment) AS total_sector_investment
      FROM ATOM_ENTITY_SECTOR_ALLOCATION_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
    , goal AS (SELECT investor_id, AVG(avg_volatility_pct) AS avg_volatility_pct FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTMENT_GOAL_001 GROUP BY investor_id), 
    scen AS (
      SELECT investor_id,
             COUNT(*) AS scenario_count,
             AVG(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS avg_scenario_change,
             MAX(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS max_scenario_change
      FROM ATOM_EVENT_SCENARIO_REBALANCING_001
      GROUP BY investor_id
    )
     SELECT p.investor_id, p.investor_name, ROUND(h.risk_score + alloc.max_allocation_pct + goal.avg_volatility_pct - h.liquidity_score + scen.avg_scenario_change,2) AS risk_pressure_score FROM ATOM_ENTITY_INVESTOR_PROFILE_001 p JOIN ATOM_ENTITY_PORTFOLIO_HEALTH_001 h ON h.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN alloc ON alloc.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN goal ON goal.investor_id=p.investor_id JOIN scen ON scen.investor_id=p.investor_id ORDER BY risk_pressure_score DESC LIMIT 10;

             SUM(CASE WHEN date >= '2025-01-01' AND date < '2026-01-01' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS net_cash_flow_2025

    )

             AVG(new_allocation_pct-current_allocation_pct) AS avg_scenario_change,
